▶ integration-core on PostgreSQL 17 (BE-02, API side)
  ▶ webhook transaction (25.7, 38.2 item 13)
    ✔ a verified message is stored under the vendor event ID and its dispatch job is enqueued in the same transaction (72.520523ms)
    ✔ a duplicate gets 200 and enqueues nothing; nothing new is stored (4.964388ms)
    ✔ concurrent duplicates: exactly one insert, one job (85.880858ms)

▶ notifications on PostgreSQL 17 (BE-06, API side)
  ▶ templates (22.13, RA-R1-05, BD-10)
    ✔ a template binding a service, diagnosis, medicine or clinician name cannot be saved - service layer and database (68.045894ms)
    ✔ render check then publish: needs the check, needs every configured language, publishes the whole version, retires the old one (222.932668ms)
    ✔ only the admin realm writes templates; staff and patients read them (T-CONF); anonymous reads none (65.990296ms)
  ✔ templates (22.13, RA-R1-05, BD-10) (357.912921ms)
  ▶ send, dedupe, cancel (22.13, 24, BR-08)
    ✔ send binds allowlisted values only: one queued row per channel, a job per row in the same transaction, no address, no text (68.755196ms)
    ✔ free text, unknown names, a service or clinician field, bad links and condition-revealing location names never reach a notification (TC-NT-06) (27.715348ms)
    ✔ dedupe keys prevent double sends: a repeat creates nothing and queues nothing, also under concurrency (163.633231ms)
    ✔ a rolled-back business transaction leaves no notification and no job; a delayed send waits for send_after (29.790163ms)
    ✔ cancel by dedupe-key prefix cancels queued rows of that resource only; sent rows are final (93.825079ms)
    ✔ in-app notices: a patient lists only their own sent notices and marks them read once (19.732697ms)
  ✔ send, dedupe, cancel (22.13, 24, BR-08) (943.067706ms)
  ▶ row-level security and grants
    ✔ app_api: patients see only their own in-app rows; staff (google) and admin see no patient notification; anonymous sees nothing (60.685821ms)
    ✔ inserts: a patient only for themselves, queued only; admin realm only to principals; a worker needs its patients scope (11.945611ms)
    ✔ the application role cannot change content, delete, or touch receipts; receipts and reads are append-only (9.607752ms)
    ✔ table rules: dedupe key without the recipient, recipient kind consistent, values on the allowlist, forward-only status (8.509251ms)
  ✔ row-level security and grants (91.131683ms)
  ▶ merge and export participants (22.16, 24, SBR-16)
    ✔ repointPatient: R rows move to the survivor, K rows stay, counts add up for notifications, receipts and reads; scope is enforced (66.136911ms)
    ✔ exportPart returns the patient's notifications, receipts and reads - no address and no message text exist to leak (4.733675ms)
    ✔ the participants register with the kernel registries once (0.442601ms)
  ✔ merge and export participants (22.16, 24, SBR-16) (71.51176ms)
✔ notifications on PostgreSQL 17 (BE-06, API side) (5289.126973ms)
